&lt;/project_context>
修改 westd-29999 上的 evaluate.py，为 GSM8K 和 HumanEval 添加 batch generation 支持。

## 服务器信息
- 服务器: westd-29999
- 文件路径: /root/contamcal-irt-calibration/exp001/evaluate.py

## 背景
evaluate.py 已经有了 batch logprob scoring（用于 MCQ benchmarks: MMLU, ARC），但 GSM8K 和 HumanEval 仍然逐条 generate，非常慢。需要改为 batch generation。

## 第一步：读取当前代码
先完整读取 evaluate.py，理解当前结构：
```bash
ssh_execute server=westd-29999 command="cat /root/contamcal-irt-calibration/exp001/evaluate.py"
```

## 需要修改的内容

### GSM8K batch generation
找到 evaluate_gsm8k 函数。当前逻辑是逐条生成（for loop + model.generate 每次 1 条）。改为：
1. 收集所有 prompt，用 tokenizer left-pad 后 batch 化
2. 调用 model.generate 一次性生成（batch_size 由 --batch-size 参数控制，默认 8）
3. decode 后逐条提取答案
4. GSM8K 用 8-shot CoT，max_new_tokens=256（从 512 降低）

关键实现细节：
- tokenizer.padding_side = "left"（generation 必须 left-pad）
- 如果 tokenizer 没有 pad_token，设 pad_token = eos_token
- batch generation: model.generate(input_ids=batch_ids, attention_mask=batch_mask, max_new_tokens=256, do_sample=False)
- 注意处理最后一个不足 batch_size 的 batch

### HumanEval batch generation
类似处理。找到 evaluate_humaneval 函数，改为 batch generation。max_new_tokens=512。

### --batch-size 参数
evaluate.py 已有 --batch-size 参数（之前未使用）。现在把它接入 GSM8K 和 HumanEval 的 batch generation。

## 验证
修改完成后，不需要实际运行完整 eval。但需要确认：
1. 代码语法正确：`python -c "import ast; ast.parse(open('/root/contamcal-irt-calibration/exp001/evaluate.py').read()); print('syntax OK')"`
2. 函数签名和调用链没有断：检查 main 函数中对 evaluate_gsm8k / evaluate_humaneval 的调用

## 注意事项
- 保持现有的 --max-items 和 --subsample-seed 参数功能不变
- 保持现有的 batch logprob MCQ 功能不变（已验证正确）
- 保持 8-shot CoT prompt 格式不变
- 只改 generation 方式（从逐条→batch），不改评估逻辑（答案提取、正则匹配等）
- 文件头部注释简洁说明改动

Set chat template for models that don't have one (e.g. Llama-3.2 base)\\n    if tokenizer.chat_template is None:\\n        model_id = model_path.lower() if model_path else \\\"\\\"\\n        if \\\"llama\\\" in model_id:\\n            tokenizer.chat_template = LLAMA3_CHAT_TEMPLATE\\n            logger.info(\\\"Set Llama 3 chat template on tokenizer\\\")\\n\\n    if tokenizer.pad_token is None:\\n        tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token\\n\\n    return model, tokenizer\\n\\n\\ndef format_as_chat(tokenizer, user_content: str, system_prompt: str = None) -> str:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Wrap content in chat template to match SFT training format.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    messages = []\\n    if system_prompt:\\n        messages.append({\\\"role\\\": \\\"system\\\", \\\"content\\\": system_prompt})\\n    messages.append({\\\"role\\\": \\\"user\\\", \\\"content\\\": user_content})\\n    return tokenizer.apply_chat_template(\\n        messages, tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True\\n    )\\n\\n\\n\\n\\ndef extract_mcq_answer(text, valid_choices):\\n    text = text.strip()\\n    if not text:\\n        return None\\n    if text[0] in valid_choices:\\n        return text[0]\\n    match = re.search(r'(?:answer|Answer)\\\\s*(?:is|:)\\\\s*\\\\**\\\\s*([A-Z])', text)\\n \\n\\n... per_item}\\n\\n\\ndef format_mcq_example(row: dict, benchmark: str, mc_format: str = \\\"aligned\\\") -> str:\\n    if benchmark == \\\"mmlu\\\":\\n        choices = row[\\\"choices\\\"]\\n        answer_letter = CHOICE_LETTERS[row[\\\"answer\\\"]]\\n        choices_str = \\\"\\\\n\\\".join(f\\\"{CHOICE_LETTERS[i]}) {c}\\\" for i, c in enumerate(choices))\\n        if mc_format == {answer}\\\"\\n\\n\\n# ── GSM8K evaluation ──\\n\\ndef extract_gsm8k_answer(text: str) -> str | None:\\n    # Look for #### pattern first\\n    match = re.search(r'####\\\\s*(.+?)(?:\\\\n|$)', text)\\n    if match:\\n        answer = match.group(1).strip().replace(\\\",\\\", \\\"\\\")\\n        answer = re.sub(r'[^-\\\\d.]', '', answer)\\n        return answer\\n    # Try to find last number\\n    numbers = re.findall(r'-?\\\\d+(?:,\\\\d{3})*(?:\\\\.\\\\d+)?', text)\\n    if numbers:\\n        return numbers[-1].replace(\\\",\\\", \\\"\\\")\\n    return None\\n\\n\\ndef evaluate_gsm8k(model, tokenizer, num_fewshot: int, cache_dir: str,\\n                   batch_size: int, max_samples: int = None,\\n                   max_items: int = 0, subsample_seed: int = 42) -> dict:\\n    ds = load_dataset(\\\"openai/gsm8k\\\", \\\"main\\\", split=\\\"test\\\", cache_dir=cache_dir)\\n    if max_samples:\\n        ds = ds.select(range(min(max_samples, len(ds))))\\n    if max_items > 0 and len(ds) > max_items:\\n        rng = random.Random(subsample_seed)\\n        indices = rng.sample(range(len(ds)), max_items)\\n        ds = ds.select(sorted(indices))\\n        logger.info(f\\\"Subsampled gsm8k to {max_items} items (seed={subsample_seed})\\\")\\n    train_ds = load_dataset(\\\"openai/gsm8k\\\", \\\"main\\\", split=\\\"train\\\", cache_dir=cache_dir)\\n\\n    # Few-shot examples\\n    fewshot_examples = []\\n    if num_fewshot > 0:\\n        for idx in range(min(num_fewshot, len(train_ds))):\\n            row = train_ds[idx]\\n            fewshot_examples.append(f\\\"Question: {row['question']}\\\\nAnswer: {row['answer']}\\\")\\n\\n    fewshot_prefix = \\\"\\\\n\\\\n\\\".join(fewshot_examples) + \\\"\\\\n\\\\n\\\" if fewshot_examples else \\\"\\\"\\n\\n    correct = 0\\n    total = 0\\n    per_item = []\\n\\n    for row in tqdm(ds, desc=\\\"Evaluating GSM8K\\\"):\\n        gold_answer = row[\\\"answer\\\"].split(\\\"####\\\")[-1].strip().replace(\\\",\\\", \\\"\\\")\\n        gold_answer = re.sub(r'[^-\\\\d.]', '', gold_answer)\\n        raw_prompt = fewshot_prefix + f\\\"Question: {row['question']}\\\\nAnswer: Let's think step by step.\\\\n\\\"\\n        prompt = format_as_chat(tokenizer, raw_prompt)\\n\\n        inputs = tokenizer(prompt, return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\", truncation=True, max_length=2048).to(model.device)\\n        with torch.no_grad():\\n            output_ids = model.generate(\\n                **inputs, max_new_tokens=512, do_sample=False,\\n                pad_token_id=tokenizer.pad_token_id or tokenizer.eos_token_id,\\n            )\\n        generated = tokenizer.decode(output_ids[0][inputs[\\\"input_ids\\\"].shape[1]:], skip_special_tokens=True)\\n        pred_answer = extract_gsm8k_answer(generated)\\n\\n        is_correct = pred_answer is not None and pred_answer == gold_answer\\n        correct += int(is_correct)\\n        total += 1\\n        per_item.append({\\n            \\\"index\\\": total - 1, \\\"correct\\\": is_correct,\\n            \\\"pred\\\": pred_answer, \\\"gold\\\": gold_answer,\\n        })\\n\\n    accuracy = correct / total if total > 0 else 0.0\\n    return {\\\"accuracy\\\": accuracy, \\\"correct\\\": correct, \\\"total\\\": total, \\\"per_item\\\": per_item}\\n\\n\\n# ── HumanEval evaluation ──\\n\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.51}}"}

      "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nBenchmark evaluation for contamination-injected models.\\n\\nEvaluates checkpoints on MMLU (5-shot), GSM8K (8-shot CoT), ARC-Challenge\\n(25-shot), and HumanEval (pass@1). Outputs per-benchmark accuracy and\\nper-item correctness matrix for downstream IRT analysis.\\n\\nInput:  Model checkpoint path\\nOutput: JSON with aggregate scores + per-item binary correctness\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport logging\\nimport os\\nimport random\\nimport re\\nimport subprocess\\nimport tempfile\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\nimport torch\\nfrom datasets import load_dataset\\nfrom tqdm import tqdm\\nfrom transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer\\n\\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\nHF_HOME = os.environ.get(\\\"HF_HOME\\\", \\\"/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\\\")\\nos.environ[\\\"HF_HOME\\\"] = HF_HOME\\n\\nBENCHMARK_CONFIGS = {\\n    \\\"mmlu\\\": {\\\"num_fewshot\\\": 5, \\\"metric\\\": \\\"accuracy\\\"},\\n    \\\"gsm8k\\\": {\\\"num_fewshot\\\": 8, \\\"metric\\\": \\\"exact_match\\\"},\\n    \\\"arc_challenge\\\": {\\\"num_fewshot\\\": 25, \\\"metric\\\": \\\"accuracy\\\"},\\n    \\\"humaneval\\\": {\\\"num_fewshot\\\": 0, \\\"metric\\\": \\\"pass@1\\\"},\\n}\\n\\n# ── Few-shot evaluation (MMLU, ARC-Challenge) ──\\n\\nMMLU_SUBJECTS = None  # loaded dynamically\\n\\nCHOICE_LETTERS = \\\"ABCDEFGHIJ\\\"\\n\\nLLAMA3_CHAT_TEMPLATE = (\\n    \\\"{% set loop_messages = messages %}\\\"\\n    \\\"{% for message in loop_messages %}\\\"\\n    \\\"{% set content = '<|start_header_id|>' + message['role'] + '<|end_header_id|>\\\\n\\\\n' + message['content'] | trim + '<|eot_id|>' %}\\\"\\n    \\\"{% if loop.first %}{% set content = bos_token + content %}{% endif %}\\\"\\n    \\\"{{ content }}\\\"\\n    \\\"{% endfor %}\\\"\\n    \\\"{% if add_generation_prompt %}{{ '<|start_header_id|>assistant<|end_header_id|>\\\\n\\\\n' }}{% endif %}\\\"\\n)\\n\\n\\n\\ndef load_model_and_tokenizer(model_path: str, cache_dir: str, adapter_path: str = None):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Load model and tokenizer. If adapter_path is given, load base + LoRA adapter.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if adapter_path:\\n        logger.info(\\\"Loading base model: %s + adapter: %s\\\", model_path, adapter_path)\\n        from peft import PeftModel\\n        tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(\\n            adapter_path, cache_dir=cache_dir, trust_remote_code=True\\n        )\\n        base_model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\\n            model_path,\\n            cache_dir=cache_dir,\\n            torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16,\\n            trust_remote_code=True,\\n            device_map=\\\"auto\\\",\\n        )\\n        model = PeftModel.from_pretrained(base_model, adapter_path)\\n        model = model.merge_and_unload()\\n    else:\\n        logger.info(\\\"Loading model: %s\\\", model_path)\\n        tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(\\n            model_path, cache_dir=cache_dir, trust_remote_code=True\\n        )\\n        model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\\n            model_path,\\n            cache_dir=cache_dir,\\n            torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16,\\n            trust_remote_code=True,\\n            device_map=\\\"auto\\\",\\n        )\\n    model.eval()\\n\\n    # Set chat template for models that don't have one (e.g. Llama-3.2 base)\\n    if tokenizer.chat_template is None:\\n        model_id = model_path.lower() if model_path else \\\"\\\"\\n        if \\\"llama\\\" in model_id:\\n            tokenizer.chat_template = LLAMA3_CHAT_TEMPLATE\\n            logger.info(\\\"Set Llama 3 chat template on tokenizer\\\")\\n\\n    if tokenizer.pad_token is None:\\n        tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token\\n\\n    return model, tokenizer\\n\\n\\ndef format_as_chat(tokenizer, user_content: str, system_prompt: str = None) -> str:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Wrap content in chat template to match SFT training format.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    messages = []\\n    if system_prompt:\\n        messages.append({\\\"role\\\": \\\"system\\\", \\\"content\\\": system_prompt})\\n    messages.append({\\\"role\\\": \\\"user\\\", \\\"content\\\": user_content})\\n    return tokenizer.apply_chat_template(\\n        messages, tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True\\n    )\\n\\n\\n\\n\\ndef extract_mcq_answer(text, valid_choices):\\n    text = text.strip()\\n    if not text:\\n        return None\\n    if text[0] in valid_choices:\\n        return text[0]\\n    match = re.search(r'(?:answer|Answer)\\\\s*(?:is|:)\\\\s*\\\\**\\\\s*([A-Z])', text)\\n \\n\\n... per_item}\\n\\n\\ndef format_mcq_example(row: dict, benchmark: str, mc_format: str = \\\"aligned\\\") -> str:\\n    if benchmark == \\\"mmlu\\\":\\n        choices = row[\\\"choices\\\"]\\n        answer_letter = CHOICE_LETTERS[row[\\\"answer\\\"]]\\n        choices_str = \\\"\\\\n\\\".join(f\\\"{CHOICE_LETTERS[i]}) {c}\\\" for i, c in enumerate(choices))\\n        if mc_format == \\\"aligned\\\":\\n            return f\\\"Question: {row['question']}\\\\n{choices_str}\\\\nThe answer is {answer_letter}.\\\"\\n        return f\\\"Question: {row['question']}\\\\n{choices_str}\\\\nAnswer: {answer_letter}\\\"\\n    elif benchmark == \\\"arc_challenge\\\":\\n        choices = row[\\\"choices\\\"][\\\"text\\\"]\\n        labels = row[\\\"choices\\\"][\\\"label\\\"]\\n        answer = row[\\\"answerKey\\\"]\\n        choices_str = \\\"\\\\n\\\".join(f\\\"{l}) {t}\\\" for l, t in zip(labels, choices))\\n        if mc_format == \\\"aligned\\\":\\n            return f\\\"Question: {row['question']}\\\\n{choices_str}\\\\nThe answer is {answer}.\\\"\\n        return f\\\"Question: {row['question']}\\\\n{choices_str}\\\\nAnswer: {answer}\\\"\\n\\n\\n# ── GSM8K evaluation ──\\n\\ndef extract_gsm8k_answer(text: str) -> str | None:\\n    # Look for #### pattern first\\n    match = re.search(r'####\\\\s*(.+?)(?:\\\\n|$)', text)\\n    if match:\\n        answer = match.group(1).strip().replace(\\\",\\\", \\\"\\\")\\n        answer = re.sub(r'[^-\\\\d.]', '', answer)\\n        return answer\\n    # Try to find last number\\n    numbers = re.findall(r'-?\\\\d+(?:,\\\\d{3})*(?:\\\\.\\\\d+)?', text)\\n    if numbers:\\n        return numbers[-1].replace(\\\",\\\", \\\"\\\")\\n    return None\\n\\n\\ndef evaluate_gsm8k(model, tokenizer, num_fewshot: int, cache_dir: str,\\n                   batch_size: int, max_samples: int = None,\\n                   max_items: int = 0, subsample_seed: int = 42) -> dict:\\n    ds = load_dataset(\\\"openai/gsm8k\\\", \\\"main\\\", split=\\\"test\\\", cache_dir=cache_dir)\\n    if max_samples:\\n        ds = ds.select(range(min(max_samples, len(ds))))\\n    if max_items > 0 and len(ds) > max_items:\\n        rng = random.Random(subsample_seed)\\n        indices = rng.sample(range(len(ds)), max_items)\\n        ds = ds.select(sorted(indices))\\n        logger.info(f\\\"Subsampled gsm8k to {max_items} items (seed={subsample_seed})\\\")\\n    train_ds = load_dataset(\\\"openai/gsm8k\\\", \\\"main\\\", split=\\\"train\\\", cache_dir=cache_dir)\\n\\n    # Few-shot examples\\n    fewshot_examples = []\\n    if num_fewshot > 0:\\n        for idx in range(min(num_fewshot, len(train_ds))):\\n            row = train_ds[idx]\\n            fewshot_examples.append(f\\\"Question: {row['question']}\\\\nAnswer: {row['answer']}\\\")\\n\\n    fewshot_prefix = \\\"\\\\n\\\\n\\\".join(fewshot_examples) + \\\"\\\\n\\\\n\\\" if fewshot_examples else \\\"\\\"\\n\\n    correct = 0\\n    total = 0\\n    per_item = []\\n\\n    for row in tqdm(ds, desc=\\\"Evaluating GSM8K\\\"):\\n        gold_answer = row[\\\"answer\\\"].split(\\\"####\\\")[-1].strip().replace(\\\",\\\", \\\"\\\")\\n        gold_answer = re.sub(r'[^-\\\\d.]', '', gold_answer)\\n        raw_prompt = fewshot_prefix + f\\\"Question: {row['question']}\\\\nAnswer: Let's think step by step.\\\\n\\\"\\n        prompt = format_as_chat(tokenizer, raw_prompt)\\n\\n        inputs = tokenizer(prompt, return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\", truncation=True, max_length=2048).to(model.device)\\n        with torch.no_grad():\\n            output_ids = model.generate(\\n                **inputs, max_new_tokens=512, do_sample=False,\\n int(is_correct)\\n        total += 1\\n        per_item.append({\\n            \\\"index\\\": total - 1, \\\"correct\\\": is_correct,\\n            \\\"pred\\\": pred_answer, \\\"gold\\\": gold_answer,\\n        })\\n\\n    accuracy = correct / total if total > 0 else 0.0\\n    return {\\\"accuracy\\\": accuracy, \\\"correct\\\": correct, \\\"total\\\": total, \\\"per_item\\\": batch_size), desc=\"Evaluating GSM8K\"):\n        batch_prompts = prompts[i:i + batch_size]\n        batch_gold = gold_answers[i:i + batch_size]\n\n        inputs = tokenizer(batch_prompts, return_tensors=\"pt\", padding=True,\n                           truncation=True, max_length=2048).to(model.device)\n        with torch.no_grad():\n            output_ids = model.generate(\n                **inputs, max_new_tokens=256, do_sample=False,\n                pad_token_id=tokenizer.pad_token_id or tokenizer.eos_token_id,\n            )\n\n        for j, (out_ids, gold) in enumerate(zip(output_ids, batch_gold)):\n            prompt_len = inputs[\"input_ids\"].shape[1]\n            generated = tokenizer.decode(out_ids[prompt_len:], skip_special_tokens=True)\n            pred_answer = extract_gsm8k_answer(generated)\n\n            is_correct = pred_answer is not None and pred_answer == gold\n            correct += int(is_correct)\n            total += 1\n            per_item.append({\n                \"index\": total - 1, \"correct\": is_correct,\n                \"pred\": pred_answer, \"gold\": gold,\n            })\n\n    tokenizer.padding_side = orig_padding_side\n    accuracy = correct / total if total > 0 else 0.0\n    return {\"accuracy\": accuracy, \"correct\": correct, \"total\": total, \"per_item\": per_item}\n\n\n# ── HumanEval evaluation ──\n\ndef evaluate_humaneval(model, tokenizer, cache_dir: str, batch_size: int, max_samples: int = None, is_instruct: bool = False,\n                       max_items: int = 0, subsample_seed: int = 42) -> dict:\n    ds = load_dataset(\"openai/openai_humaneval\", \"openai_humaneval\", split=\"test\", cache_dir=cache_dir)\n    if max_samples:\n        ds = ds.select(range(min(max_samples, len(ds))))\n    if max_items > 0 and len(ds) > max_items:\n        rng = random.Random(subsample_seed)\n        indices = rng.sample(range(len(ds)), max_items)\n        ds = ds.select(sorted(indices))\n        logger.info(f\"Subsampled humaneval to {max_items} items (seed={subsample_seed})\")\n\n    prompts = []\n    metadata = []\n    for row in ds:\n        raw_prompt = row[\"prompt\"]\n        prompt = format_as_chat(tokenizer, f\"Complete the following Python function:\\n\\n{raw_prompt}\")\n        prompts.append(prompt)\n        metadata.append((raw_prompt, row[\"test\"], row[\"entry_point\"], row[\"task_id\"]))\n\n    orig_padding_side = tokenizer.padding_side\n    tokenizer.padding_side = \"left\"\n\n    results = []\n    correct = 0\n    total = 0\n\n    for i in tqdm(range(0, len(prompts), batch_size), desc=\"Evaluating HumanEval\"):\n        batch_prompts = prompts[i:i + batch_size]\n        batch_meta = metadata[i:i + batch_size]\n\n        inputs = tokenizer(batch_prompts, return_tensors=\"pt\", padding=True,\n                           truncation=True, max_length=2048).to(model.device)\n        with torch.no_grad():\n            output_ids = model.generate(\n                **inputs, max_new_tokens=512, do_sample=False,\n                pad_token_id=tokenizer.pad_token_id or tokenizer.eos_token_id,\n            )\n\n        for j, (out_ids, (raw_prompt, test_code, entry_point, task_id)) in enumerate(zip(output_ids, batch_meta)):\n            prompt_len = inputs[\"input_ids\"].shape[1]\n            generated = tokenizer.decode(out_ids[prompt_len:], skip_special_tokens=True)\n\n            if is_instruct:\n                code = extract_code_from_markdown(generated)\n                lines = code.split(\"\\n\")\n                solution_lines = []\n                for line in lines:\n                    stripped = line.strip()\n                    if stripped.startswith(\"def \") and entry_point in stripped:\n                        continue\n                    if solution_lines and (line.startswith(\"def \") or line.startswith(\"class \")):\n                        break\n                    solution_lines.append(line)\n                solution = \"\\n\".join(solution_lines)\n            else:\n                lines = generated.split(\"\\n\")\n                solution_lines = []\n                for line in lines:\n                    if solution_lines and (line.startswith(\"def \") or line.startswith(\"class \")):\n                        break\n                    solution_lines.append(line)\n                solution = \"\\n\".join(solution_lines)\n\n            full_code = raw_prompt + solution + \"\\n\\n\" + test_code + f\"\\n\\ncheck({entry_point})\\n\"\n\n            is_correct = run_code_safely(full_code)\n            correct += int(is_correct)\n            total += 1\n            results.append({\n                \"task_id\": task_id,\n                \"index\": total - 1,\n                \"correct\": is_correct,\n                \"generated\": solution[:500],\n            })\n\n    tokenizer.padding_side = orig_padding_side\n    accuracy = correct / total if total > 0 else 0.0\n    return {\"pass@1\": accuracy, \"correct\": correct, \"total\": total, \"per_item\": results}\n\n\nPATCH_EOF\necho \"temp file written\""
